# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# [124049] CHI | 1914 | Hunan Declares Independence! (¡Hunan declara su independencia!)
# Trigger event 124037 in events/CHI/CHI_13-33.txt
# Trigger event 124041 in events/GLOBAL/ALL_13-14.txt
# Trigger event 124079 in events/GLOBAL/ALL_13-14.txt
# Trigger event 309014 in events/Minors/UCH.txt
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
event = {
	id = 124049
	random = no
	country = CHI
	trigger = {
		or = {
			or = {
				and = {
					not = {
						area = { area = "Hunan" country = U74 data = 1 value = 70 }
					}
					exists = U74
				}
				and = {
					not = {
						area = { area = "Hunan" country = UCH data = 1 value = 70 }
					}
					exists = UCH
				}
			}
			random = 3
		}
		or = {
			and = {
				war = { country = CHI country = U74 }
				exists = U74
				not = { exists = UCH }
			}
			and = {
				war = { country = CHI country = UCH }
				exists = UCH
				not = { exists = U74 }
			}
		}
	}
	name = EVT_CHI_124049_NAME
	desc = EVT_CHI_124049_DESC
	picture = "GLOBAL/ccip_beiyangarmy"
	style = 2
	date = { day = 0 month = 5 year = 1914 }
	offset = 5
	deathdate = { day = 1 month = january year = 1918 }
	action = {
		name = ACTION_NAME_HURRAH
		command = { type = secedearea which = CHI value = "Hunan" when = 2 }
		command = { trigger = { exists = U74 } type = event where = U74 which = 124079 when = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { exists = UCH } type = event where = UCH which = 124079 when = 1 }
		command = { type = add_corps which = "Hunan Difangjun" value = land where = 1308 }
		command = { trigger = { division_exists = { type = 22003 id = 10111 } } type = activate_division which = 22003 value = 10111 when = 1 where = 1308 }
		command = { trigger = { not = { division_exists = { type = 22003 id = 10111 } } } type = add_division which = "Hunan Lujun" value = infantry when = 2 }
	}
}
